# 📚 AI & Dev tools catalog

> A unified catalog of **GitHub repositories** and **websites/services** collected from
> reels and links saved in chat.
> **1145 repositories** + **89 websites**, grouped by practical category.
> Activity status checked on **2026-11-07**. Legend: 🟢 active (pushed ≤12 months ago) · 🟡 slowing down · 🔴 stalled · ⚫ archived · 🌐 website.

## Index
- **Coding agents, Claude Code & AI-assisted development** (183)
- **AI agent frameworks & personal assistants** (128)
- **LLMs, models & local inference** (99)
- **RAG, agent memory & knowledge bases** (48)
- **OCR & document parsing** (28)
- **Media generation (video, images, 3D, voice)** (202)
- **Security & supply chain** (67)
- **Dev tools, productivity & libraries** (404)
- **AI finance & trading** (16)
- **AI research, world models & vector data** (59)

| [Skills di Matt Pocock](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills) | Matt Pocock's collection of engineering skills for coding agents; among them /retro, which runs a retrospective on how the agent worked (navigation, mechanical checks, standards, rules in CLAUDE.md/AGENTS.md) and proposes turning rules into deterministic checks. | When you want to improve the Claude Code or Codex working environment starting from what went wrong in sessions. | 🟢 ⭐317.1k · 2026-11-05 · MIT |

| [Runable](https://runable.com) | General-purpose AI agent platform that plans and executes tasks end-to-end: generates sites, videos, slides, images, apps and workflows from a single subscription. | When you want an all-purpose agent that produces complete deliverables without you assembling the toolchain. | 🌐 site |
| [Aside](https://aside.com) | Browser with a built-in agent that works on sites where the user already has an open session, carrying out tasks on their behalf. Claims local data processing and a password manager that authenticates without exposing credentials to the agent. | When automation has to go through authenticated sites that don't expose an API. | 🌐 site |
| [Glasser](https://glasser.ai) | Pay-as-you-go API for AI agents: a single key and a skill give Claude Code, Cursor and Codex access to web search, company data and SEO data, with the price shown before every call. | When an agent needs premium data from multiple providers without signing up for separate subscriptions. | 🌐 site |
| [Okara AI](https://okara.ai) | Commercial 'AI CMO' platform that automates SEO, optimization for AI search engines (GEO), content and presence on Reddit, LinkedIn and X. | When you evaluate automated marketing agents for SEO and GEO. | 🌐 site |
| [Manus](https://manus.im) | General-purpose AI agent that, among other things, turns a description of a site into a working full-stack application you refine through conversation. | When you want a web application prototype starting from a description. | 🌐 site |
| [Clef (Cloudflare)](https://huggingface.co/Cloudflare/clef) | Open-weights decision models (Clef and Clef-flash, Apache 2.0) that don't generate text but answer typed questions about a state (text, code, logs, images) with calibrated probabilities in a single pass; also available on Workers AI. | When an agent needs to make fast classification or routing decisions before calling an LLM. | 🌐 site |
| [ManyChat](https://manychat.com) | Commercial messaging automation platform for Instagram, Messenger and WhatsApp through Meta's official APIs; it includes a Claude block for replying to DMs with generated text. | When you want to automate replies to Instagram direct messages. | 🌐 site |
| [Lindy](https://www.lindy.ai) | Commercial platform for building AI automation agents from templates: email replies, bookings, Slack digests, meeting assistant, lead notifications. | When you want to automate repetitive office tasks without writing code. | 🌐 site |

## LLMs, models & local inference

| Project | What it does | When to use it | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| [Ollama](https://github.com/ollama/ollama) | Runtime for running LLMs locally (Llama, Qwen, Gemma, DeepSeek, Kimi and others) with one command, importing GGUF models via Modelfile and exposing a local API; supports NVIDIA, AMD and Intel GPUs. | When you want to run a model locally and connect it to agents or applications without paid APIs. | 🟢 ⭐186.2k · 2026-11-07 · MIT |
| [Open WebUI](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui) | Self-hosted web interface for LLMs, compatible with Ollama and OpenAI-style APIs, with chat, RAG over documents, user management and plugins. | When you want a ChatGPT-like interface for local models or your own APIs, hosted in-house. | 🟢 ⭐158k · 2026-11-07 · to be verified (GitHub: NOASSERTION, non-standard license) |
| [vLLM](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm) | High-throughput, memory-efficient inference and serving engine for LLMs, with PagedAttention, continuous batching and an OpenAI-compatible API. | When you need to serve open models in production with many concurrent requests. | 🟢 ⭐96.5k · 2026-11-07 · Apache-2.0 |
| [OmniRoute](https://github.com/diegosouzapw/OmniRoute) | MIT AI gateway that unifies hundreds of LLM providers behind a single endpoint, with automatic fallback and token compression. | When you run out of tokens with a provider and want automatic routing and fallback across many models. | 🟢 ⭐85.3k · 2026-11-07 · MIT |
| [LLM Course](https://github.com/mlabonne/llm-course) | Course for getting into Large Language Models, with a roadmap and Colab notebooks on fundamentals, training, fine-tuning and deployment. | When you want to study LLMs in an orderly way, with hands-on exercises. | 🟢 ⭐84.9k · 2026-02-05 · Apache-2.0 |
| [Unsloth AI](https://github.com/unslothai/unsloth) | Train LLMs in VS Code + Colab | When you want to fine-tune LLMs (Llama, Mistral, etc.) quickly and with little VRAM. | 🟢 ⭐79.1k · 2026-11-07 · Apache-2.0 |
| [LiteLLM](https://github.com/BerriAI/litellm) | AI gateway with a Rust core and Python SDK that exposes over 100 LLM APIs in OpenAI format, with cost tracking, guardrails, load balancing and logging. | When you need to unify access to multiple LLM providers behind a single API with cost control. | 🟢 ⭐62.9k · 2026-11-07 · to be verified (GitHub: NOASSERTION, non-standard license) |

Apache-2.0. | When you need offline tool calling on phones, home automation or screenless devices, for short, clear commands. | 🟢 ⭐1 · 2026-11-05 |

| [Jev (TypeSafe AI)](https://typesafe.ai) | Commercial model in early access that generates decisions rather than text: you pass it a state and the closed set of allowed answers, and it returns a typed value with a probability and confidence score, designed to be consumed by code. | [MixRoute](https://mixroute.ai) | LLM gateway that exposes Claude, GPT, Gemini, DeepSeek and over 250 models behind a single OpenAI-compatible API key, with no price markup and automatic failover. | When you want to access many models with a single key and a single base URL. | 🌐 site |
| [Liquid AI d1](https://huggingface.co/LiquidAI/d1-3B) | Liquid AI's family of open models for decisions (null, choice, score outputs): d1-3B for text and images, d1-omni-600M also for audio; weights on Hugging Face and the full model via API. | When you need fast decisions or classifications over a closed set of options with a small, local model. | 🌐 site |
| [OpenAI Decisions API](https://developers.openai.com/api/docs/guides/decisions) | OpenAI API for real-time decisions over a predefined set of answers, with predicate, choice and score outputs and a dedicated playground. | When you need fast, cheap classifications or choices inside an application or an agent. | 🌐 site |
| [Uncensured AI](https://uncensuredai.com) | Italian provider of open source models with refusal filters removed, usable from a web interface or via an OpenAI-compatible API. | When you need an LLM for security or code analysis in a lab and commercial models refuse the request. | 🌐 site |
| [Kolibri-1 (Aleph Alpha)](https://huggingface.co/Aleph-Alpha/Kolibri-1) | Open-weights Mixture-of-Experts model with 78B total and about 3.5B active parameters, focused on German and English, with a reasoning mode, tool calling and context up to about 1M tokens; Apache 2.0 license. | When you're looking for a European open-weights model for agents in regulated environments. | 🌐 site |
| [Nemotron 3.5 Bolt E4B (NVIDIA)](https://huggingface.co/nvidia/NVIDIA-Nemotron-3.5-Bolt-E4B-BF16) | Compact NVIDIA model (about 4B effective parameters, derived from Gemma 4 E4B) optimized for local agents with tool calling, which takes up a few GB when quantized and can run on CPU and ordinary RAM. | When you want a small agentic model to run on a laptop or mini PC without a GPU. | 🌐 site |
| [Abliteration.ai](https://docs.abliteration.ai/models) | API service (OpenAI-compatible) for abliterated models, i.e. with the refusal directions removed from the weights, keeping tool calling; it connects to clients such as Cline or Claude Code. | When an agent needs to work on security tasks that standard models refuse. | 🌐 site |
| [Liquid AI Models (LFM)](https://www.liquid.ai/models) | Catalog of Liquid Foundation Models, a non-transformer architecture with linear memory, including 24B variants with agentic capabilities designed to run on CPU and system RAM. | When you're looking for efficient models for local CPU inference without a dedicated GPU. | 🌐 site |
| [Qwen Chat](https://chat.qwen.ai) | Alibaba's free assistant in the browser and in an app: chat, image and video generation, web pages with preview, deep research and document analysis; private, non-commercial use only under its terms. | When you want to try multimodal features for free, without confidential data. | 🌐 site |
| [Flint (Recursive)](https://huggingface.co/RecursiveSI/flint) | Open-weights coding model from Recursive: a 40B-parameter mixture-of-experts with about 2.5B active per token, 262K context, tool use and agents; Apache 2.0 license, designed to run locally. | When you want a fast local coding model to use with an agent without usage-based costs. | 🌐 site |
| [Experiential](https://platform.experientiallabs.ai) | API access platform for third-party models, with a playground and observability; among others it offers Gemini 4 Argon, which you can try for free. | When you want to compare a new model with the one you usually use on the same prompts. | 🌐 site |
| [LFM2.5-24B-A2B (Liquid AI)](https://huggingface.co/LiquidAI/LFM2.5-24B-A2B) | Liquid AI open-weights reasoning model with 24B parameters and about 2.4B active, 131K-token context, designed for local agents; LFM Open License v1.0, supported by llama.cpp, MLX and vLLM. | When you want an agentic model that runs fast on a laptop. | 🌐 site |
| [Muse Spark Open (Meta)](https://huggingface.co/meta-models/Muse-Spark-Open-816B-A42B) | Meta's open-weight multimodal model (816 billion total parameters, 42 billion active, MoE architecture) that accepts text and images; Hugging Face hosts the weights, an NVFP4 version and community GGUF/MLX conversions. Custom Meta license. | When you're looking for a high-end open model to serve on your own infrastructure. | 🌐 site |
| [Emma-6 (Egomnia)](https://huggingface.co/egomnia/emma-6) | Small experimental Italian language model from Egomnia (about 1.55 billion parameters, 4,096-token context), trained on Italian text only; the weights are on Hugging Face under an OpenRAIL license. Public tests show frequent errors even on simple questions. | When you want to study or compare a small model trained only in Italian, not for production use. | 🌐 site |
| [Google AI Studio](https://aistudio.google.com) | Google's web environment for trying Gemini models, getting API keys and building web apps by describing them in natural language. | When you want to prototype with Gemini or get a free API key. | 🌐 site |

| [Headroom](https://github.com/headroomlabs-ai/headroom) | Local context compression layer: -60/95% tokens sent to LLMs (Netflix engineer T. Chopra) | Compress context sent to LLMs and cut token costs by 60-95%. | 🟢 ⭐79.1k · 2026-11-07 · Apache-2.0 |

## OCR & document parsing

|---|---|---|---|

## Security & supply chain

| Project | What it does | When to use it | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| [REA](https://github.com/morluto/rea) | Reverse engineering toolkit for AI agents via MCP and CLI: decompiles native apps and binaries, traces execution while preserving evidence and helps recreate a feature in your stack. Works with Claude, Codex, Cursor and Gemini CLI, locally. | When an agent needs to analyze an app or binary without source code and explain how it works with evidence. | 🟢 ⭐439.4k · 2026-11-07 · MIT |
| [Clash Verge Rev](https://github.com/clash-verge-rev/clash-verge-rev) | Tauri graphical client for Mihomo/Clash-based proxies, for Windows, macOS and Linux: visual management of profiles and rules, customizable interface and settings sync via WebDAV. | When you need to manage proxy rules and traffic routing through an interface instead of configuration files. | 🟢 ⭐156.9k · 2026-11-07 · GPL-3.0 |
